## 🤔 Seedance 2.0 是什么？

**Seedance 2.0** 是 **字节跳动** 开发的视频生成模型，是业界首个支持 **图片、视频、音频、文本四模态同时输入** 的模型。

**核心特性：**

* 🎥 **文生视频** — 根据文字描述生成视频
* 🖼️ **图生视频** — 将静态图片动画化
* 📹 **视频转视频** — 变换和扩展现有视频
* 🎵 **音频驱动** — 通过音频输入驱动视频生成
* 📐 **最高 1080p 分辨率**，4–15 秒时长
* 🔊 **自动配音配乐** — 自动生成旁白和背景音乐

### Raw found footage style, amateur handheld smartphone video recording a real…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
Raw found footage style, amateur handheld smartphone video recording a real intense battle between superhuman fighters, heavy natural camera shake and jitter from the operator's unsteady hands and movement, low-fi consumer camera quality with visible grain, compression artifacts, motion blur during fast pans, single continuous uncut take with no edits or cuts, paparazzi-style close but cautious recording in broad daylight on an open rocky terrain with dust and scattered debris, natural lighting, realistic reactions from the camera as it follows the chaotic action.
No external references. Maintain perfect visual consistency for both fighters' exact appearance, hair, body type, clothing and proportions throughout the entire 15-second continuous clip.
0-3s: [Continuous Shaky Handheld] The shaky phone captures a bald fighter with a third eye on his forehead, white headband and green gi facing a tall athletic woman with long black hair in a high ponytail and red battle armor. They circle each other. The woman dashes in with fast kicks. The bald fighter blocks and counters with precise palm strikes. The camera shakes with every exchange.
3-6s: The bald fighter fires a bright triangular energy blast from his forehead. The woman dodges at the last second and the blast explodes against the ground. She immediately counters with a spinning kick to his side. The handheld footage shakes hard from the explosion and the operator moves quickly to stay with the action.
6-9s: The woman grabs the bald fighter's arm and throws him over her shoulder. He flips and lands on his feet, then fires a rapid volley of energy blasts from both hands. The woman weaves between them with incredible agility. The camera pans and tilts constantly with heavy natural shake and motion blur.
9-12s: The bald fighter charges and they clash in close combat again. The woman lands a powerful knee to his stomach and follows with an elbow to the back of his head. The shaky camera moves in closer, the operator clearly excited and breathing hard.
12-15s: The bald fighter staggers but recovers and fires one final powerful blast. The woman crosses her arms and blocks it, the energy pushing her back several meters and kicking up dust. The continuous footage keeps rolling as the dust settles and both fighters catch their breath, the camera still shaking naturally.
Raw consumer found footage aesthetic, heavy handheld instability and micro-shakes, natural motion blur from camera movement, realistic lens flare and slight overexposure on energy flashes, coherent superhuman physics and impacts visible through dust and debris, amateur recording feel with no stabilization, no text, no overlays, no watermarks, continuous single shot, documentary raw video style.
```

### 15 seconds, single continuous handheld selfie vlog POV, energetic pacing, no…

#### 📝 提示词

```
15 seconds, single continuous handheld selfie vlog POV, energetic pacing, no hard cuts.
Global setup: High energy viral content-creator video. Wide angle vlog lens, natural handheld shake, bright vibrant lighting like a well-lit room or studio setup with ring light glow. Setting: a cozy content-creator room desk with a laptop, ring light, phone tripod, colorful LED strip lighting in the background. Subject: a young content creator, casual trendy outfit, expressive and excited energy, holding the camera at arm's length. Ambient sound: upbeat viral trending audio, cash/paper rustling sound effects, excited laughter.

[0:03–0:06] She holds the camera up, grinning, says "Guys... I think I just went viral," just as the first few dollar bills start fluttering down from above into frame.
[0:06–0:09] More cash begins raining down steadily around her, she laughs, throws her hands up, spins slightly in her chair, says "IT'S RAINING MONEY RIGHT NOW, LOOK!"
[0:09–0:12] Full money shower fills the frame, bills fluttering everywhere, she grabs handfuls into the air and lets them fall, laughing uncontrollably, genuinely thrilled reaction.
[0:12–0:15] She catches her breath, looks at the camera with a huge grin, bills still gently falling around her, says "Content really does pay off," winks, freeze on her laughing face. Fade to black.
Style: vibrant saturated color grade, high energy viral aesthetic, natural realistic cash physics (fluttering, not floating unnaturally), no text overlays, realistic expressive acting. Constraints: consistent character appearance and outfit throughout, physically plausible falling motion for the bills, no distorted hands or face, natural lip-sync to dialogue, energetic but believable pacing.
```

### 参照写真の女性本人を忠実に再現し、10秒の超リアルな2000年代後半の日本のガラケー動画風ホームビデオを作成。シームレスなワンショットではなく、2〜3秒ごとに明…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 提示词

```
参照写真の女性本人を忠実に再現し、10秒の超リアルな2000年代後半の日本のガラケー動画風ホームビデオを作成。シームレスなワンショットではなく、2〜3秒ごとに明確に切り替わる4ショット構成にすること。

全体を通して、顔立ち、髪型、髪色、体型、年齢感、服装、雰囲気は参照写真に忠実に保ち、同一人物として一貫性を維持すること。美化しすぎず、自然体で、日常の中の一瞬を古い携帯電話で何気なく撮ったような空気感にする。

舞台は日本の静かな住宅地にある、築年数の少しある普通の家の室内。午前遅めの自然光。玄関、細い廊下、小さなリビング、木の床、ローテーブル、薄いカーテン、すりガラスの窓、生活感のある棚、古い壁、家庭的な小物、扇風機や時計、室内植物などがある。本当に人が住んでいる日本の家に見えること。豪華な家、モデルルーム、カフェ風、広告風、高級インテリアは禁止。

映像は2000年代後半の日本のガラケーで撮った低画質ホームビデオ風。古い携帯電話の内蔵カメラで、友人や家族が何気なく撮ったような映像。低解像度、低ビットレート、強いデジタル圧縮ノイズ、少しカクついたフレーム感、白飛びしやすい窓辺、暗部のザラつき、色のにじみ、弱いダイナミックレンジ、こもった音声、手ブレ、雑な構図、ピントの甘さを入れる。綺麗な4K映像、映画風、Vlog風、Instagram風レトロフィルター、VHS風、フィルム風、広告風の画づくりは禁止。

Shot1（00:00–00:02.5）
玄関から廊下につながる場所。彼女は玄関の段差付近でしゃがみ、脱いだスリッパや靴の向きを軽くそろえる。そのあと自然に立ち上がりかけ、少しだけ笑う。カメラは少し手ブレし、ピントが甘く、室内の暗さで少しノイズが出る。このショットの終わりで明確にカット。

Shot2（00:02.5–00:05.0）
小さなリビングの窓際。彼女は薄いカーテンを少しだけ開けて外を見る。窓の明るさで画面が少し白飛びし、露出が不安定になる。風でカーテンが少し揺れ、彼女はそれを軽く押さえる。構図は少しズレていて、撮影者が何気なく携帯を向けた感じ。このショットの終わりでカット。

Shot3（00:05.0–00:07.5）
ローテーブルのある小さなリビング。彼女は床か座布団に座り、テーブルに置かれたマグカップのそばで静かにくつろいでいる。大きな動きはせず、窓の外の音に気づいて視線だけを少し上げる。薄いカーテンが風でわずかに揺れ、室内の光が少し変化する。カメラは手ブレしながら、彼女の顔に少し遅れてピントを合わせる。低解像度のガラケー動画らしく、暗部にノイズがあり、細部は少し潰れている。このショットの終わりでカット。

Shot4（00:07.5–00:10.0）
細い廊下かリビングの入口付近。彼女はマグカップを片手にゆっくり歩いてくる。画面外の誰かに呼ばれ、少し振り向いて軽く手を上げ、自然に短く「はーい」または「なに？」と返す。カメラはその反応を少し遅れて捉え、フレームが少しズレる。最後は動きの途中で突然ブラックアウトし、古い携帯電話の録画を止めたように終わる。

音声は自然音のみ。遠くの車の小さな走行音、鳥の声、風、カーテンの擦れる音、床を歩く足音、食器やマグカップの小さな音、家の中の静かな生活音。音声は少しこもっていて、古い携帯電話で録ったようにする。音楽、ナレーション、映画的効果音は禁止。

禁止事項：美肌フィルター、完璧すぎる肌、モデル風ポーズ、広告っぽいツヤ感、K-POPアイドル風演出、映画的照明、スローモーション、滑らかすぎるカメラ、高級住宅、モデルルーム風、完璧すぎる構図、演技っぽいリアクション、幻想表現、過度なエモ演出、字幕、テキスト、ロゴ、ウォーターマークは禁止。
```

### A young Korean woman (22), naturally beautiful with realistic skin texture…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
A young Korean woman (22), naturally beautiful with realistic skin texture, minimal makeup, soft black shoulder-length hair loosely tied back with face-framing strands. She wears an oversized cream knit sweater over a white tank top, loose olive cargo pants, worn black Converse sneakers, and a simple silver necklace. Maintain identical identity, clothing, hairstyle, facial features, body proportions, and appearance throughout the video.

Story Concept

A quiet Sunday morning where she leaves home with an old film camera to photograph ordinary things most people ignore. The video feels like someone casually following their friend without planning anything.

Nothing is performed.

Nothing dramatic happens.

Every moment feels accidentally beautiful.

Location

A peaceful Korean hillside residential neighborhood with narrow stairways, ivy-covered brick walls, tiny gardens, old mailboxes, bicycles, rooftop terraces, flowering plants spilling over fences, utility poles, overhead electrical wires, old apartment buildings, and soft summer sunlight filtering through large trees.

No cafés.

No stores.

No advertisements.

No crowds.

No modern architecture.

Visual Style

Hyper-realistic documentary.

Feels like authentic Korean family footage recorded in 2002.

Completely unscripted.

Slow everyday rhythm.

Tiny imperfections make everything believable.

Camera

Sony MiniDV camcorder.

Heavy handheld shake.

Frequent accidental reframing.

Late reactions.

Autofocus breathing.

Rolling shutter.

Exposure pumping.

Motion blur during movement.

Occasional zoom mistakes.

Slight tape compression.

CCD sensor noise.

Faded colors.

Soft contrast.

No cinematic shots.

No stabilization.

Timeline

00:00–00:03

She steps outside carrying a small vintage film camera around her neck. She pauses to photograph sunlight shining through leaves. The camcorder operator almost misses the shot while adjusting focus.

00:03–00:06

Walking down a narrow staircase, she notices colorful flowers growing through a cracked wall. She kneels to photograph them, smiling quietly to herself.

00:06–00:09

She finds an old orange neighborhood cat sleeping beside parked bicycles. She slowly reaches out to pet it. The cat lazily stretches while autofocus repeatedly shifts between them.

00:09–00:12

She sits on worn concrete steps eating a strawberry ice cream. A drop melts onto her hand and she laughs naturally before wiping it away with her sleeve.

00:12–00:15

She walks uphill toward home. Halfway she notices the camcorder, smiles with slight embarrassment, gives a tiny wave, then continues walking. Recording suddenly stops mid-step as if the tape ended.

Audio

Only authentic neighborhood sounds.

Birds singing.

Wind through trees.

Footsteps.

Distant scooters.

Children playing far away.

Leaves rustling.

Film camera shutter clicks.

Cat purring.

Electric wires humming.

No music.

No narration.

No sound effects.

Goal

Create footage that feels genuinely discovered inside an old family camcorder from South Korea in 2002—warm, imperfect, nostalgic, deeply human, and so realistic that viewers question whether it is AI-generated or a real forgotten memory.
```

#### 📝 提示词

```
PART 1 – THE REALITY (15s)

15s • 16:9 • Ultra-photorealistic • Broadcast football cinematography • World Cup stadium at night

Shot 1 (0–3s) – The Pressure

```
Main Subject

Young Filipino woman, early 20s, from a rural province. Warm medium-tan skin with natural texture, minimal to no makeup. Long straight black hair tied into a relaxed low ponytail with a few loose strands framing her face. She wears a faded floral sleeveless blouse, comfortable knee-length cotton shorts, simple rubber slippers (tsinelas), and a small woven bracelet. Friendly, humble, cheerful, and approachable. Maintain the exact same facial features, hairstyle, clothing, and overall appearance throughout the entire video.

Location

Authentic rural Filipino barangay during a peaceful late morning. Narrow concrete and dirt roads, nipa huts and modest concrete homes with galvanized metal roofs, coconut trees, banana plants, bamboo fences, flowering plants, hanging laundry, sari-sari store in the distance, parked bicycles, water drums, plastic pails, bamboo benches, roaming chickens and dogs, tricycles occasionally passing, and lush tropical surroundings. Quiet neighborhood with genuine everyday life.

Visual Style

Photorealistic nostalgic home-video realism. Warm tropical atmosphere. Genuine unscripted family-memory feeling. Everyday provincial life captured naturally with believable human behavior. Rich environmental details, humid air, realistic lighting, and authentic Filipino culture.

Camera Style

1990s VHS camcorder aesthetic.

Soft analog image, slightly washed-out colors, visible VHS scan lines, tape grain, mild tracking distortion near the bottom of the frame, slight color bleeding, occasional white balance shifts, handheld family-video shakiness, imperfect zooms, slow autofocus, slight overexposure under harsh sunlight, timestamp-style recording feel (without visible text), authentic analog imperfections, no cinematic movements, no stabilization, no modern color grading.

TIMED SCENES (00:00–00:15)

00:00–00:02

Outside a modest provincial home with coconut trees nearby. She sits on a bamboo bench fixing her ponytail while smiling softly. A gentle tropical breeze moves loose strands of hair as the VHS camera briefly loses focus before recovering.

00:02–00:04

She walks along a quiet barangay road carrying a small plastic tabo. A native chicken crosses her path. She pauses and laughs naturally while watching it. The handheld camera follows imperfectly with slight zoom adjustments.

00:04–00:06

She playfully tries to guide the chicken away from the road using gentle hand motions. The chicken runs into the yard while she laughs. Bright sunlight filters through coconut leaves, producing natural flickering shadows. Focus shifts between her and the chicken.

00:06–00:08

In front of her house, she picks up a traditional Filipino broom (walis tingting) and sweeps fallen leaves from the concrete yard. Fine dust and dry leaves move naturally. The camera exposure changes slightly as clouds pass overhead.

00:08–00:10

She sits back on the bamboo bench after sweeping. Using a small towel (pamunas), she wipes sweat from her forehead and quietly watches children playing farther down the barangay road. Gentle handheld drift gives the feeling of a family member recording.

00:10–00:12

Someone off-camera greets her with a cheerful "Magandang umaga!" She turns toward the voice, smiles warmly, waves casually, and replies, "Magandang umaga rin!" The camera catches her reaction a moment late due to natural handheld movement.

00:12–00:15

She walks slowly beneath coconut and mango trees carrying a small plastic pail. She notices the camera, gives a shy smile, then continues walking toward the neighborhood. The VHS recording abruptly cuts to black mid-step, as though someone pressed the stop button on the camcorder.

Audio

Natural ambient sound only—birds singing, distant rooster crows, rustling coconut leaves, light wind, neighborhood conversations, dogs barking occasionally, footsteps on concrete and dirt, broom sweeping, chickens clucking, insects, and subtle tropical village ambience. No music. No narration. No cinematic sound effects.

Goal

An authentic Filipino provincial home video from the 1990s that feels like a real family memory—warm, nostalgic, imperfect, and deeply believable. Simple everyday moments of a young Filipino woman living peacefully in her rural barangay, captured with the charm of an old VHS camcorder.
```

A stone platform at an ancient post station on the edge of a high mountain canyon. On the opposite side of the canyon, a dead tree extends from the edge of a cliff. Rolling clouds cover the depths of the canyon. A crosswind blows from left to right, causing the hem of the character’s clothing, the goshawk’s feathers, and the short stem beneath the apple to sway in the wind.
STYLE
A modern stylized Chinese 3D animated film look, combined with Disney animation’s cinematic character presentation.

Low-angle close shot, 28mm.

Sound effects: the goshawk’s wingbeats, its talons scraping across the wooden table, canyon wind.
SHOT 2
Medium close shot, 40mm.
The view shifts from the goshawk to the master archer. The master archer immediately turns around and takes two steps forward, raises the longbow with his left hand, and pulls a red-feathered arrow from the quiver on his back with his right hand. The hem of his clothing is blown behind him by the wind. He looks toward the sky above the canyon, and his gaze continuously follows the apple swinging beneath the goshawk’s talon.
Sound effects: footsteps, the sound of pulling out the arrow, fabric moving in the wind.
SHOT 3
Wide side view of the canyon, 24mm.
The camera follows the goshawk from the side as it flies. The goshawk spreads its wings and flies through the canyon, its body tilting slightly in the crosswind. The red apple hangs beneath its body and swings forward and backward as it flies.
In the distance, the master archer walks to the edge of the stone platform, places his front foot near the cliff edge of the platform, and then stands firmly.
Sound effects: canyon wind, the goshawk’s wingbeats.
SHOT 4
Medium close shot from the front side, 50mm.
The master archer places the red-feathered arrow on the bowstring, extends his left arm straight forward, and pulls the bowstring behind his ear with his right hand. His waist and back remain steady, and the bow moves slightly with the goshawk’s position in the air.
The goshawk continues flying forward. The apple swings beneath its talon and gradually moves into the open space below the talon and wings.
Sound effects: the bow bending under tension, breathing, wind.
SHOT 5
Side close shot, 135mm telephoto.
When the apple swings directly beneath the goshawk’s body, the master archer releases the bowstring. The red-feathered arrow flies across the canyon, passes beneath the goshawk’s spread wings, and pierces through the center of the red apple.
The apple leaves the goshawk’s talon and continues flying forward with the arrow. The goshawk is not injured and continues flapping away in its original direction.
Sound effects: the bowstring snapping, the arrow cutting through the air, the dull sound of the apple being pierced.
SHOT 6
135mm telephoto following the arrow, then switching to a wide view of the canyon.
The red-feathered arrow carries the whole apple toward the opposite side and embeds itself in the main trunk of the dead tree. The end of the arrow continues vibrating in front of the tree trunk, with the apple fixed on the arrow shaft.
The goshawk safely passes above the dead tree and continues flying into the distance. The view returns to a wide shot of the canyon. The master archer stands at the edge of the stone platform and calmly lowers the longbow, while the hem of his clothing spreads in the crosswind.
The master archer is on the nearby stone platform, the apple on the dead tree across the canyon is clearly visible, and the goshawk appears farther away in the sky.
Sound effects: the arrowhead embedding into the tree trunk, the end of the arrow vibrating, the goshawk calling, one deep drumbeat.

Quiet local atmosphere with no shops, cafés, advertisements, crowds, or commercial activity.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary-style footage. Authentic everyday behavior and natural body language. Unscripted slice-of-life energy. Strong environmental realism with rich neighborhood details, believable human movement, and genuine lived-in atmosphere.

00:06–00:08

00:08–00:10
Seated on a quiet terrace with a ceramic coffee cup, she watches the neighborhood peacefully. She occasionally brushes hair behind her ear. Loose handheld side-angle framing with natural camera drift.
00:10–00:12
Close side profile. Someone off-camera greets her. She turns, raises a hand, smiles warmly, and casually says, “Annyeong.” The camera reacts slightly late, capturing the moment imperfectly.
00:12–00:15
She walks slowly along a tree-lined residential lane holding her coffee cup. Noticing the camera, she gives a brief genuine smile before looking away and continuing down the street. The recording abruptly cuts to black mid-motion, as if the camcorder has been switched off.

Audio: Natural ambient sound only. Morning birds, distant motorcycles, soft wind, rustling leaves, faint neighborhood voices, occasional cat sounds, footsteps on concrete, laundry moving on clotheslines, and subtle residential ambience. No music, narration, or added sound effects.

Goal: A forgotten home-video recording from an early-2000s Korean neighborhood—warm, candid, imperfect, deeply authentic, and convincingly real.

No stabilization, no smoothness.  00:00–00:01 Hard whip-pan onto his hands slapping a bucket, motion smeared. 00:01–00:02 Snap cut to his face, mid-grin, head bobbing to the beat. 00:02–00:03 Cut to bare feet tapping cracked pavement, dust puffing. 00:03–00:04 Whip up to a balcony where a neighbor leans out, clapping. 00:04–00:05 Cut back — his hands blur across the cajón, tracking lines rolling. 00:05–00:06 Fast tilt to sunlight flaring off a teal wall behind him. 00:06–00:07 Close cut on his wrist beads snapping with each strike. 00:07–00:08 Jump to a wider frame — a second kid runs in and joins on a bucket. 00:08–00:09 Whip-pan to a dog trotting past, then back, missing the beat. 00:09–00:10 Cut to his face turning to the camera, laughing. 00:10–00:11 Snap to hands, then feet, then hands again — rapid triple cut. 00:11–00:12 Tilt down to the anklet bouncing as he shifts weight. 00:12–00:13 He calls out off-camera, points a drumstick, shouts “¡Dale!” 00:13–00:14 Whip to a woman in a doorway swaying, tape dropout flickers. 00:14–00:15 Cut back tight on the drum, sweat flying off his hand. 00:15–00:16 Fast zoom-in that overshoots, autofocus hunts on his grin. 00:16–00:17 He looks straight into the lens, still drumming, and winks. 00:17–00:18 Abrupt cut to VHS static and blue screen as the tape jams.  Audio: Natural ambient sound only — hand drums on plastic and wood, sandals scuffing pavement, clapping, a dog barking, distant salsa from an open window, shouts and laughter, a passing bicycle bell, cicada hum in the heat. No added music track. No sound design. No narration.  Goal: A blast of spontaneous street rhythm captured like a battered VHS tape found in a shoebox — fast, glitchy, sun-drenched, chaotic, and bursting with life.

镜头四（5–6.5秒）：过肩摔 — 狼型角色头部后仰回正后怒吼，双臂抓住哥布林角色手臂将其拉近，以体型优势完整执行过肩摔将哥布林角色从肩上翻摔砸向地面，哥布林角色背部重击石板地面产生地面辐射状裂纹与尘土爆散，Arcane风格冲击波从落地点向外炸开，俯拍镜头捕捉哥布林角色落地全过程 —

镜头五（6.5–8秒）：地面反击膝击 — 哥布林角色落地瞬间双腿夹住狼型角色腰部将其拉倒，两人扭打至地面，哥布林角色从下方连续以肘部猛击狼型角色面部两次，每肘落点血粒飞散冲击波爆出，狼型角色以膝盖顶住哥布林角色腹部向上猛力顶击，哥布林角色腹部受膝击身体向上弹起，Arcane风格向上圆形冲击波爆散，侧面贴地极低角度镜头 —

In a telekinetic action scene, a South Korean high school girl in a white short-sleeved shirt and gray pleated skirt stands calmly in the middle of a corridor. She is being attacked from both sides; some attackers, wearing brown school uniform jackets, wield short sticks, while others are dressed as black ninjas, creating a tense standoff in the corridor. As the enemies rush towards her, the girl unleashes a powerful telekinetic attack. Suddenly, everything around her enters a state of weightlessness and time freezes. Dozens of items—open textbooks, notebooks, a red soda can, and a wooden school chair—leave the ground, perfectly suspending themselves in mid-air. The attackers, in their lunging movements, are frozen in mid-air by an invisible force. Paper pages, scraps of paper, and dust float and swirl around her weightlessly. The camera slowly zooms in on the girl, perfectly freezing the chaotic corridor in the telekinetic-induced state of stillness and suspension. In the extreme stillness of the slowly zooming-in, frozen image, the girl's calm eyes narrow slightly. Immediately afterward, she calmly raised her right hand, her fingers loosely clenched, and then made a light yet decisive flick of her sleeve to either side of her body. In an instant, frozen time and gravity seemed to explode with this subtle gesture. A low rumble echoed in the air, and dozens of objects that had been suspended in mid-air—a heavy wooden school chair, scattered textbooks, even the red soda can—transformed into a dense projection, carrying powerful air currents, and were precisely launched towards the enemies attacking from both sides. At the same time, an invisible circular psychic shockwave violently spread out from the girl. The brown-uniformed attackers and black-clad ninjas, who had been frozen in mid-air, were instantly struck by this immense force, their bodies jolted violently, and they were sent flying backward like kites with broken strings to either end of the corridor. As gravity instantly returned, the wooden chairs shattered upon impact, textbooks scattered into a flurry of pages, and with the dull thud of heavy objects hitting the ground, the attackers collapsed onto the corridor floor, incapacitated. Silence returned to the corridor, broken only by countless tattered papers and fine dust drifting slowly in the air. The aftershocks of the shockwave ruffled the girl's bangs and hair. She remained composed, not even glancing at her fallen opponent. The girl slowly lowered her right hand, raised her slender left, and gracefully and calmly tucked a strand of black hair behind her ear, revealing her delicate and serene profile. The camera froze on this moment, the white papers falling around her like snowflakes, highlighting her still figure in a white short-sleeved shirt and gray pleated skirt. Then, the image faded out.

### In Dreamina, go to Video Generation, select the Seedance 2.0 model, and enable…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
In Dreamina, go to Video Generation, select the Seedance 2.0 model, and enable JSON mode using the Normal/JSON toggle in the prompt panel.  
Use a storyboard workflow: generate consistent storyboard frames in ChatGPT Image 2.0 first, then use those images as references for Seedance 2.0 image-to-video generation.  
For Seedance 2.0 image-to-video, upload the corresponding storyboard frame for each scene, paste a JSON prompt, validate the JSON syntax, then generate and iterate by adjusting the JSON based on a test run.  
Set Seedance 2.0 clip duration to 5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation shots.  
Save storyboard frames with consistent filenames (e.g., Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg) and review them as a slideshow before generating video to catch continuity issues early.  
When editing the generated clips, trim the first and last 0.5 seconds of each clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve transitions for smooth scene changes and Hard Cut transitions for sharp cuts.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing to make AI-generated changes feel intentional.  
Color grade with dark blue-purple tones for night scenes and warm gold tones for dawn scenes to maintain cinematic consistency.
```

#### 💡 使用技巧

In Dreamina, go to Video Generation, select the Seedance 2.0 model, and enable JSON mode using the Normal/JSON toggle in the prompt panel.  
Use a storyboard workflow: generate consistent storyboard frames in ChatGPT Image 2.0 first, then use those images as references for Seedance 2.0 image-to-video generation.  
For Seedance 2.0 image-to-video, upload the corresponding storyboard frame for each scene, paste a JSON prompt, validate the JSON syntax, then generate and iterate by adjusting the JSON based on a test run.  
Set Seedance 2.0 clip duration to 5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation shots.  
Save storyboard frames with consistent filenames (e.g., Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg) and review them as a slideshow before generating video to catch continuity issues early.  
When editing the generated clips, trim the first and last 0.5 seconds of each clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve transitions for smooth scene changes and Hard Cut transitions for sharp cuts.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing to make AI-generated changes feel intentional.  
Color grade with dark blue-purple tones for night scenes and warm gold tones for dawn scenes to maintain cinematic consistency.

### Use an attached storyboard as the visual reference to control shot composition…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
Use an attached storyboard as the visual reference to control shot composition and continuity across the whole Seedance video  
Specify a single consistent character identity (age, ethnicity, hair, outfit) and explicitly instruct “keep the same hairstyle, outfit, and character identity throughout the video” to prevent character drift  
Lock the look by listing style constraints in the prompt (Ultra realistic documentary lifestyle, Kodak Portra 400 color grading, warm cinematic indoor lighting, natural handheld documentary camera, subtle film grain, shallow depth of field)  
Break the video into timestamped shots (e.g., SHOT 1 0–2s … SHOT 8 14–15s) with explicit framing (wide/medium/close-up) and actions to guide pacing and camera coverage  
For dialogue scenes, specify exact spoken lines and enforce language constraints (all spoken dialogue must remain in Indonesian)  
Control subtitles by instructing “display subtitles only while dialogue is spoken,” with a fixed two-line format (English first line, Simplified Chinese second line) and styling (white modern sans-serif with thin black outline)  
Define audio explicitly (home ambience, boiling water, gas stove, cooking sounds, soft TV ambience) and add “no background music” to avoid unwanted soundtrack  
Use a negative prompt to block unwanted aesthetics/artifacts (no anime/CGI/fantasy, no duplicate people, no extra fingers, no blurry face, no incorrect subtitles, no watermark/logo, no text except subtitles during dialogue)  
Set generation constraints up front (15 seconds duration, 16:9 aspect ratio) to match the intended output format
```

#### 💡 使用技巧

Use an attached storyboard as the visual reference to control shot composition and continuity across the whole Seedance video  
Specify a single consistent character identity (age, ethnicity, hair, outfit) and explicitly instruct “keep the same hairstyle, outfit, and character identity throughout the video” to prevent character drift  
Lock the look by listing style constraints in the prompt (Ultra realistic documentary lifestyle, Kodak Portra 400 color grading, warm cinematic indoor lighting, natural handheld documentary camera, subtle film grain, shallow depth of field)  
Break the video into timestamped shots (e.g., SHOT 1 0–2s … SHOT 8 14–15s) with explicit framing (wide/medium/close-up) and actions to guide pacing and camera coverage  
For dialogue scenes, specify exact spoken lines and enforce language constraints (all spoken dialogue must remain in Indonesian)  
Control subtitles by instructing “display subtitles only while dialogue is spoken,” with a fixed two-line format (English first line, Simplified Chinese second line) and styling (white modern sans-serif with thin black outline)  
Define audio explicitly (home ambience, boiling water, gas stove, cooking sounds, soft TV ambience) and add “no background music” to avoid unwanted soundtrack  
Use a negative prompt to block unwanted aesthetics/artifacts (no anime/CGI/fantasy, no duplicate people, no extra fingers, no blurry face, no incorrect subtitles, no watermark/logo, no text except subtitles during dialogue)  
Set generation constraints up front (15 seconds duration, 16:9 aspect ratio) to match the intended output format

### Have an agent/scriptwriter break your idea into shot-by-shot clips (not prose)…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
Have an agent/scriptwriter break your idea into shot-by-shot clips (not prose) so Seedance 2.0 can generate each shot consistently.  
Lock your mascot/character once as a reusable asset (face/build/wardrobe/voice) and reuse it by name across episodes to maintain continuity.  
Lock the world/environment once (city/props/setting) as reusable assets so each new shot pulls from the same base for consistent lighting and scene details.  
Generate connected shots by explicitly keeping continuity of wardrobe, lighting, and key environment elements across all shots.  
Use Seedance 2.0 to animate each still image into motion, then assemble the episode by cutting all shots together on a single editing canvas (e.g., CapCut Director Mode).
```

#### 💡 使用技巧

Have an agent/scriptwriter break your idea into shot-by-shot clips (not prose) so Seedance 2.0 can generate each shot consistently.  
Lock your mascot/character once as a reusable asset (face/build/wardrobe/voice) and reuse it by name across episodes to maintain continuity.  
Lock the world/environment once (city/props/setting) as reusable assets so each new shot pulls from the same base for consistent lighting and scene details.  
Generate connected shots by explicitly keeping continuity of wardrobe, lighting, and key environment elements across all shots.

Use a single “canvas” workflow: have an agent/script break the episode into shot-by-shot prompts, then generate each shot from the same locked character/environment assets for continuity.

### Build a consistent AI dancer identity first (same face/body/outfit…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
Build a consistent AI dancer identity first (same face/body/outfit style/energy) and reuse that model across generations to prevent face/outfit drift in Seedance 2.0 dance videos.  
Generate a strong first frame (clear outfit, pose, camera angle, lighting, location, mood) before running Seedance 2.0 so the video has a stable visual anchor.  
Create a 16-panel storyboard from the first frame to map beat-by-beat movement, then use that storyboard as guidance for Seedance 2.0 image-to-video to improve timing and smooth transitions.  
Run Seedance 2.0 via a guided image-to-video workflow (influencer model → first frame → 16-panel storyboard → motion prompt → video) instead of text-only prompting for more controlled choreography.  
Write the Seedance 2.0 video prompt to reinforce what the storyboard shows (timing, camera behavior, lighting, realism) and avoid introducing new outfits/locations/camera styles that conflict with the storyboard.  
Iterate by reviewing the generated motion, refining the storyboard, and regenerating to progressively improve continuity and choreography quality.
```

#### 💡 使用技巧

Build a consistent AI dancer identity first (same face/body/outfit style/energy) and reuse that model across generations to prevent face/outfit drift in Seedance 2.0 dance videos.

Write the Seedance 2.0 video prompt to reinforce what the storyboard shows (timing, camera behavior, lighting, realism) and avoid introducing new outfits/locations/camera styles that conflict with the storyboard.  
Iterate by reviewing the generated motion, refining the storyboard, and regenerating to progressively improve continuity and choreography quality.

#### 📝 提示词

```

Limit Seedance shots to 4–6 seconds to reduce face/motion drift  
Avoid repeating the same camera move in consecutive clips (e.g., if clip 1 dollies in, make clip 2 hold or pull back)  
Repeat the same color palette/lighting language in every Seedance prompt with no exceptions to keep clips consistent

### In Dreamina, select the Seedance 2.0 model and switch the prompt panel from…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 提示词

```
In Dreamina, select the Seedance 2.0 model and switch the prompt panel from Normal to JSON mode using the toggle.  
Use ChatGPT Image 2.0 to create a character sheet first, then reuse that same character sheet as reference to keep the same character/build consistent across every frame.  
Storyboard in ChatGPT Image 2.0 by generating frames one scene at a time from a single pasted script, then save them sequentially (Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg, etc.) and review as a slideshow before generating video.  
In Seedance 2.0 (Dreamina), use Image-to-Video by uploading the matching storyboard frame for each scene to control composition and continuity.  
Set Seedance clip duration to ~5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation shots.  
Treat the first Seedance generation as a test run, then adjust the JSON prompt/settings and regenerate for the final.  
In editing, trim the first and last 0.5 seconds off each generated clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve for smooth transitions and Hard Cut for sharp transitions when assembling Seedance clips.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing for stronger impact.  
Color grade with dark blue-purple tones for night scenes and warm gold tones for dawn scenes to unify the look.
```

#### 💡 使用技巧

In Dreamina, select the Seedance 2.0 model and switch the prompt panel from Normal to JSON mode using the toggle.  
Use ChatGPT Image 2.0 to create a character sheet first, then reuse that same character sheet as reference to keep the same character/build consistent across every frame.  
Storyboard in ChatGPT Image 2.0 by generating frames one scene at a time from a single pasted script, then save them sequentially (Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg, etc.) and review as a slideshow before generating video.  
In Seedance 2.0 (Dreamina), use Image-to-Video by uploading the matching storyboard frame for each scene to control composition and continuity.  
Set Seedance clip duration to ~5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation shots.  
Treat the first Seedance generation as a test run, then adjust the JSON prompt/settings and regenerate for the final.  
In editing, trim the first and last 0.5 seconds off each generated clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve for smooth transitions and Hard Cut for sharp transitions when assembling Seedance clips.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing for stronger impact.  
Color grade with dark blue-purple tones for night scenes and warm gold tones for dawn scenes to unify the look.
